## Supplemental full-file review: non-authoritative disagreement receipts

Every complete file was supplied in line order to the installed declarative judge. These broad full-file labels are NOT used as literal extraction or routing findings: 92 domain labels contradict exact sequential substring accounting, including the sole ordinary-word file. Low-confidence and contradictory answers are retained, not silently accepted or rewritten. No missing receiver can be inferred from these labels. The targeted O001 judgment supersedes its broad full-file label. Exact state, path/range, digest and returned probabilities follow for each file.

### Invocation/tool limitations
JavaScript judge_batch was undefined; the documented Python helper was used. An initial await on the synchronous batch constructor raised TypeError after submitting the batch. Completion notification supplied jdgb-15b3299009447e45; attach recovered that same completed batch. No duplicate batch was submitted. The execution failures are not semantic judgments. Raw-prefix read selectors were used for source to avoid suffix-selector truncation; all reconstructed source hashes except the documented pre-existing ios_developer drift match supplied inventory.

## Full-file receipt: `crates/op-agents/src/agent_catalog.rs`

### Verbatim state
```json
{
  "path": "crates/op-agents/src/agent_catalog.rs",
  "range": "1-78",
  "sha256": "7e60fb1594b25a9074976d1b787cf9962e19421d18a3fd1ea7362536b834fc4f",
  "source": "1: //! Agent catalog for tool registration.\n2: //!\n3: //! Builds descriptors from the unified registry via `create_agent`, including\n4: //! per-operation JSON Schemas.\n5: \n6: use crate::agent_schema::{AgentOperationSchema, infer_category, schemas_for_operations};\n7: use crate::unified::{self, AgentCategory};\n8: use crate::{AgentTrait, create_agent, list_agent_types};\n9: \n10: /// Minimal descriptor for tool registration + schema export.\n11: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n12: pub struct AgentDescriptor {\n13:     pub agent_type: String,\n14:     pub name: String,\n15:     pub description: String,\n16:     pub operations: Vec<String>,\n17:     pub category: AgentCategory,\n18:     /// Typed JSON Schema per operation (MCP / plugin contract).\n19:     pub operation_schemas: Vec<AgentOperationSchema>,\n20: }\n21: \n22: impl AgentDescriptor {\n23:     pub fn schema_for(&self, operation: &str) -> Option<&AgentOperationSchema> {\n24:         self.operation_schemas.iter().find(|s| s.name == operation)\n25:     }\n26: }\n27: \n28: fn describe_agent(agent: &dyn AgentTrait) -> AgentDescriptor {\n29:     let agent_type = agent.agent_type().to_string();\n30:     let operations = agent.operations();\n31:     let category = unified::GLOBAL_REGISTRY\n32:         .get_normalized(&agent_type)\n33:         .map(|a| a.category())\n34:         .unwrap_or_else(|| infer_category(&agent_type, &operations));\n35:     let description = agent.description().to_string();\n36:     let operation_schemas = schemas_for_operations(category, &operations, &description);\n37: \n38:     AgentDescriptor {\n39:         agent_type,\n40:         name: agent.name().to_string(),\n41:         description,\n42:         operations,\n43:         category,\n44:         operation_schemas,\n45:     }\n46: }\n47: \n48: /// List built-in agents suitable for MCP/tool exposure.\n49: pub fn builtin_agent_descriptors() -> Vec<AgentDescriptor> {\n50:     let mut out = Vec::new();\n51:     // Deduplicate on the *canonical* agent_type() — list ids and unified ids\n52:     // can alias the same concrete agent (e.g. go-pro / golang-pro).\n53:     let mut seen = std::collections::HashSet::new();\n54: \n55:     let mut push_created = |agent: Box<dyn AgentTrait + Send + Sync>| {\n56:         let canonical = agent.agent_type().to_string();\n57:         if seen.insert(canonical) {\n58:             out.push(describe_agent(agent.as_ref()));\n59:         }\n60:     };\n61: \n62:     for agent_type in list_agent_types() {\n63:         match create_agent(agent_type, \"catalog\".to_string()) {\n64:             Ok(agent) => push_created(agent),\n65:             Err(err) => tracing::warn!(agent_type, error = %err, \"catalog skip\"),\n66:         }\n67:     }\n68: \n69:     for id in unified::GLOBAL_REGISTRY.list_ids() {\n70:         match create_agent(id, \"catalog\".to_string()) {\n71:             Ok(agent) => push_created(agent),\n72:             Err(err) => tracing::warn!(agent = id, error = %err, \"unified catalog skip\"),\n73:         }\n74:     }\n75: \n76:     out.sort_by(|a, b| a.agent_type.cmp(&b.agent_type));\n77:     out\n78: }"
}

Deserialize)]\n92: pub struct AgentInstance {\n93:     pub id: String,\n94:     pub agent_type: String,\n95:     pub pid: Option<u32>,\n96:     pub status: AgentStatus,\n97:     pub started_at: chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,\n98:     pub last_health_check: Option<chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>>,\n99:     pub restart_count: u32,\n100: }\n101: \n102: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n103: #[serde(rename_all = \"snake_case\")]\n104: pub enum AgentStatus {\n105:     Starting,\n106:     Running,\n107:     Healthy,\n108:     Unhealthy,\n109:     Stopping,\n110:     Stopped,\n111:     Failed { reason: String },\n112: }\n113: \n114: /// Agent factory for creating agents\n115: #[async_trait]\n116: pub trait AgentFactory: Send + Sync {\n117:     /// Create a new agent instance\n118:     async fn create_agent(&self, spec: &AgentSpec, instance_id: &str) -> Result<AgentHandle>;\n119: \n120:     /// Check if an agent type is supported\n121:     fn supports(&self, agent_type: &str) -> bool;\n122: }\n123: \n124: /// Handle to a running agent (process or in-process trait object)\n125: pub enum AgentBackend {\n126:     Process(tokio::process::Child),\n127:     InProcess(std::sync::Arc<dyn crate::AgentTrait + Send + Sync>),\n128: }\n129: \n130: pub struct AgentHandle {\n131:     pub id: String,\n132:     pub backend: AgentBackend,\n133:     pub spec: AgentSpec,\n134: }\n135: \n136: impl AgentHandle {\n137:     pub fn pid(&self) -> Option<u32> {\n138:         match &self.backend {\n139:             AgentBackend::Process(child) => child.id(),\n140:             AgentBackend::InProcess(_) => None,\n141:         }\n142:     }\n143: \n144:     pub async fn execute_task(&self, task: crate::AgentTask) -> Result<crate::TaskResult> {\n145:         match &self.backend {\n146:             AgentBackend::InProcess(agent) => {\n147:                 agent.execute(task).await.map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))\n148:             }\n149:             AgentBackend::Process(_) => Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n150:                 \"Process-backed agents do not support in-process task execution\"\n151:             )),\n152:         }\n153:     }\n154: }\n155: \n156: /// In-process factory backed by `create_agent` / unified registry.\n157: pub struct InProcessAgentFactory;\n158: \n159: #[async_trait]\n160: impl AgentFactory for InProcessAgentFactory {\n161:     async fn create_agent(&self, spec: &AgentSpec, instance_id: &str) -> Result<AgentHandle> {\n162:         let agent = crate::create_agent(&spec.agent_type, instance_id.to_string())\n163:             .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n164:         Ok(AgentHandle {\n165:             id: instance_id.to_string(),\n166:             backend: AgentBackend::InProcess(std::sync::Arc::from(agent)),\n167:             spec: spec.clone(),\n168:         })\n169:     }\n170: \n171:     fn supports(&self, agent_type: &str) -> bool {\n172:         crate::create_agent(agent_type, \"probe\".to_string()).is_ok()\n173:     }\n174: }\n175: \n176: /// Default agent factory that spawns processes\n177: pub struct ProcessAgentFactory;\n178: \n179: #[async_trait]\n180: impl AgentFactory for ProcessAgentFactory {\n181:     async fn create_agent(&self, spec: &AgentSpec, instance_id: &str) -> Result<AgentHandle> {\n182:         let mut cmd = tokio::process::Command::new(&spec.command);\n183: \n184:         // Add arguments\n185:         cmd.args(&spec.args);\n186: \n187:         // Add instance ID as environment variable\n188:         cmd.env(\"AGENT_ID\", instance_id);\n189:         cmd.env(\"AGENT_TYPE\", &spec.agent_type);\n190: \n191:         // Add custom environment variables\n192:         for (key, value) in &spec.env {\n193:             cmd.env(key, value);\n194:         }\n195: \n196:         // Set working directory\n197:         if let Some(dir) = &spec.working_dir {\n198:             cmd.current_dir(dir);\n199:         }\n200: \n201:         // Spawn the process\n202:         let process = cmd\n203:             .spawn()\n204:             .context(format!(\"Failed to spawn agent: {}\", spec.command))?;\n205: \n206:         Ok(AgentHandle {\n207:             id: instance_id.to_string(),\n208:             backend: AgentBackend::Process(process),\n209:             spec: spec.clone(),\n210:         })\n211:     }\n212: \n213:     fn supports(&self, agent_type: &str) -> bool {\n214:         // Only for explicit external commands — in-process factory takes precedence.\n215:         let _ = agent_type;\n216:         false\n217:     }\n218: }\n219: \n220: /// Agent registry for managing agent specifications and instances\n221: pub struct AgentRegistry {\n222:     /// Registered agent specifications\n223:     specs: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<String, AgentSpec>>>,\n224: \n225:     /// Running agent instances\n226:     instances: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<String, AgentInstance>>>,\n227: \n228:     /// Agent factories\n229:     factories: Arc<RwLock<Vec<Box<dyn AgentFactory>>>>,\n230: \n231:     /// Agent handles\n232:     handles: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<String, AgentHandle>>>,\n233: }\n234: \n235: impl Default for AgentRegistry {\n236:     fn default() -> Self {\n237:         Self::new()\n238:     }\n239: }\n240: \n241: impl AgentRegistry {\n242:     pub fn new() -> Self {\n243:         Self {\n244:             specs: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),\n245:             instances: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),\n246:             factories: Arc::new(RwLock::new(vec![\n247:                 Box::new(InProcessAgentFactory),\n248:                 Box::new(ProcessAgentFactory),\n249:             ])),\n250:             handles: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),\n251:         }\n252:     }\n253: \n254:     /// Register an agent specification\n255:     pub async fn register_spec(&self, spec: AgentSpec) -> Result<()> {\n256:         let mut specs = self.specs.write().await;\n257: \n258:         if specs.contains_key(&spec.agent_type) {\n259:             return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n260:                 \"Agent type '{}' is already registered\",\n261:                 spec.agent_type\n262:             ));\n263:         }\n264: \n265:         specs.insert(spec.agent_type.clone(), spec);\n266:         Ok(())\n267:     }\n268: \n269:     /// Load specifications from a configuration file\n270:     pub async fn load_specs_from_file(&self, path: &PathBuf) -> Result<()> {\n271:         let content = tokio::fs::read_to_string(path)\n272:             .await\n273:             .context(\"Failed to read agent specifications file\")?;\n274: \n275:         let specs: Vec<AgentSpec> =\n276:             serde_json::from_str(&content).context(\"Failed to parse agent specifications\")?;\n277: \n278:         for spec in specs {\n279:             self.register_spec(spec).await?;\n280:         }\n281: \n282:         Ok(())\n283:     }\n284: \n285:     /// Load specifications from a directory\n286:     pub async fn load_specs_from_directory(&self, dir: &PathBuf) -> Result<()> {\n287:         let mut entries = tokio::fs::read_dir(dir)\n288:             .await\n289:             .context(\"Failed to read specifications directory\")?;\n290: \n291:         while let Some(entry) = entries.next_entry().await? {\n292:             let path = entry.path();\n293: \n294:             if path.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) == Some(\"json\")\n295:                 && let Err(e) = self.load_specs_from_file(&path).await\n296:             {\n297:                 tracing::warn!(\"Failed to load spec from {:?}: {}\", path, e);\n298:             }\n299:         }\n300: \n301:         Ok(())\n302:     }\n303: \n304:     /// Register a custom agent factory\n305:     pub async fn register_factory(&self, factory: Box<dyn AgentFactory>) {\n306:         let mut factories = self.factories.write().await;\n307:         factories.push(factory);\n308:     }\n309: \n310:     /// Spawn an agent instance\n311:     pub async fn spawn_agent(\n312:         &self,\n313:         agent_type: &str,\n314:         _config: Option<OwnedValue>,\n315:     ) -> Result<String> {\n316:         // Get the specification\n317:         let specs = self.specs.read().await;\n318:         let spec = specs\n319:             .get(agent_type)\n320:             .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!(\"Unknown agent type: {}\", agent_type))?\n321:             .clone();\n322: \n323:         // Check max instances\n324:         let instances = self.instances.read().await;\n325:         let current_count = instances\n326:             .values()\n327:             .filter(|i| {\n328:                 i.agent_type == agent_type\n329:                     && matches!(i.status, AgentStatus::Running | AgentStatus::Healthy)\n330:             })\n331:             .count();\n332: \n333:         if current_count >= spec.max_instances {\n334:             return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n335:                 \"Maximum instances ({}) reached for agent type '{}'\",\n336:                 spec.max_instances,\n337:                 agent_type\n338:             ));\n339:         }\n340:         drop(instances);\n341: \n342:         // Generate instance ID\n343:         let instance_id = format!(\"{}-{}\", agent_type, uuid::Uuid::new_v4());\n344: \n345:         // Find suitable factory\n346:         let factories = self.factories.read().await;\n347:         let factory = factories\n348:             .iter()\n349:             .find(|f| f.supports(agent_type))\n350:             .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!(\"No factory supports agent type: {}\", agent_type))?;\n351: \n352:         // Create the agent\n353:         let handle = factory.create_agent(&spec, &instance_id).await?;\n354:         let pid = handle.pid();\n355: \n356:         // Store the handle\n357:         let mut handles = self.handles.write().await;\n358:         handles.insert(instance_id.clone(), handle);\n359: \n360:         // Create instance record\n361:         let instance = AgentInstance {\n362:             id: instance_id.clone(),\n363:             agent_type: agent_type.to_string(),\n364:             pid,\n365:             status: AgentStatus::Starting,\n366:             started_at: chrono::Utc::now(),\n367:             last_health_check: None,\n368:             restart_count: 0,\n369:         };\n370: \n371:         // Store instance\n372:         let mut instances = self.instances.write().await;\n373:         instances.insert(instance_id.clone(), instance);\n374: \n375:         // TODO: Start health check task if configured\n376: \n377:         Ok(instance_id)\n378:     }\n379: \n380:     /// Kill an agent instance\n381:     pub async fn kill_agent(&self, instance_id: &str) -> Result<()> {\n382:         let mut handles = self.handles.write().await;\n383: \n384:         match handles.remove(instance_id) {\n385:             Some(mut handle) => {\n386:                 match &mut handle.backend {\n387:                     AgentBackend::Process(process) => {\n388:                         process\n389:                             .kill()\n390:                             .await\n391:                             .context(\"Failed to kill agent process\")?;\n392:                     }\n393:                     AgentBackend::InProcess(_) => {}\n394:                 }\n395: \n396:                 // Update instance status\n397:                 let mut instances = self.instances.write().await;\n398:                 if let Some(instance) = instances.get_mut(instance_id) {\n399:                     instance.status = AgentStatus::Stopped;\n400:                 }\n401: \n402:                 Ok(())\n403:             }\n404:             _ => Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n405:                 \"Agent instance '{}' not found\",\n406:                 instance_id\n407:             )),\n408:         }\n409:     }\n410: \n411:     /// Execute a task on a running in-process agent instance\n412:     pub async fn execute_instance_task(\n413:         &self,\n414:         instance_id: &str,\n415:         task: crate::AgentTask,\n416:     ) -> Result<crate::TaskResult> {\n417:         let handles = self.handles.read().await;\n418:         let handle = handles\n419:             .get(instance_id)\n420:             .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!(\"Agent instance '{}' not found\", instance_id))?;\n421:         handle.execute_task(task).await\n422:     }\n423: \n424:     /// Get agent instance status\n425:     pub async fn get_instance_status(&self, instance_id: &str) -> Result<AgentInstance> {\n426:         let instances = self.instances.read().await;\n427:         instances\n428:             .get(instance_id)\n429:             .cloned()\n430:             .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!(\"Agent instance '{}' not found\", instance_id))\n431:     }\n432: \n433:     /// List all agent instances\n434:     pub async fn list_instances(&self) -> Vec<AgentInstance> {\n435:         let instances = self.instances.read().await;\n436:         instances.values().cloned().collect()\n437:     }\n438: \n439:     /// List all registered agent types\n440:     pub async fn list_agent_types(&self) -> Vec<String> {\n441:         let specs = self.specs.read().await;\n442:         specs.keys().cloned().collect()\n443:     }\n444: \n445:     ///   "source": "1: //! Shared natural-language advise helpers for domain agents.\n2: //!\n3: //! Operations like `advise` / `consult` / `explain` / `recommend` must answer the\n4: //! caller's question by routing into a real specialized `analyze` operation —\n5: //! never return a static capability card or canned advice stub.\n6: \n7: use simd_json::prelude::*;\n8: \n9: /// True when the operation is a natural-language advice alias.\n10: pub fn is_advise_op(op: &str) -> bool {\n11:     matches!(\n12:         op,\n13:         \"advise\" | \"consult\" | \"explain\" | \"recommend\" | \"analyze\" | \"help\"\n14:     )\n15: }\n16: \n17: /// Extract a user-facing query string from free-form args (raw string or JSON).\n18: pub fn extract_query(args: Option<&str>) -> String {\n19:     let raw = args.unwrap_or(\"\").trim();\n20:     if raw.is_empty() {\n21:         return String::new();\n22:     }\n23:     if raw.starts_with('{') {\n24:         let mut bytes = raw.as_bytes().to_vec();\n25:         if let Ok(v) = simd_json::to_owned_value(&mut bytes) {\n26:             for key in [\"query\", \"question\", \"prompt\", \"input\", \"text\", \"message\"] {\n27:                 if let Some(s) = v.get(key).and_then(|x| x.as_str())\n28:                     && !s.trim().is_empty()\n29:                 {\n30:                     return s.trim().to_string();\n31:                 }\n32:             }\n33:         }\n34:     }\n35:     raw.to_string()\n36: }\n37: \n38: /// Keyword-route a free-form advise query onto a more specific operation name.\n39: pub fn route_advise_to_op(query: &str, routes: &[(&str, &str)], default_op: &str) -> String {\n40:     let q = query.to_lowercase();\n41:     for (needle, op) in routes {\n42:         if q.contains(needle) {\n43:             return (*op).to_string();\n44:         }\n45:     }\n46:     default_op.to_string()\n47: }"
}

crate::security::{SecurityProfile, profiles::presets};\n8: \n9: const ALLOWED_DIRS: &[&str] = &[\"/tmp\", \"/home\", \"/opt\"];\n10: \n11: pub struct CodeReviewerAgent {\n12:     agent_id: profile: presets::code_reviewer(),\n21:         }\n22:     }\n23: \n24:     fn search_code(&self, path: Option<&str>, pattern: Option<&str>) -> Result<String, String> {\n25:         let mut cmd = Command::new(\"rg\");\n26: \n27:         if let Some(p) = pattern {\n28:             validation::validate_args(p)?;\n29:             cmd.arg(p);\n30:         } else {\n31:             return Err(\"Pattern required\".to_string());\n32:         }\n33: \n34:         if let Some(dir) = path {\n35:             let validated_path = validation::validate_path(dir, ALLOWED_DIRS)?;\n36:             cmd.arg(validated_path);\n37:         }\n38: \n39:         cmd.arg(\"--no-heading\").arg(\"-n\");\n40: \n41:         let output = cmd.output().map_err(|e| format!(\"Failed: {}\", e))?;\n42:         let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);\n43:         let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);\n44: \n45:         Ok(format!(\"Search results:\\n{}\\n{}\", stdout, stderr))\n46:     }\n47: \n48:     fn count_lines(&self, path: Option<&str>) -> Result<String, String> {\n49:         let mut cmd = Command::new(\"tokei\");\n50: \n51:         if let Some(dir) = path {\n52:             let validated_path = validation::validate_path(dir, ALLOWED_DIRS)?;\n53:             cmd.arg(validated_path);\n54:         }\n55: \n56:         let output = cmd.output().map_err(|e| format!(\"Failed: {}\", e))?;\n57:         let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);\n58:         let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);\n59: \n60:         Ok(format!(\"Line counts:\\n{}\\n{}\", stdout, stderr))\n61:     }\n62: \n63:     fn git_diff(&self, path: Option<&str>, args: Option<&str>) -> Result<String, String> {\n64:         let mut cmd = Command::new(\"git\");\n65:         cmd.arg(\"diff\");\n66: \n67:         if let Some(a) = args {\n68:             validation::validate_args(a)?;\n69:             for arg in a.split_whitespace() {\n70:                 cmd.arg(arg);\n71:             }\n72:         }\n73: \n74:         if let Some(dir) = path {\n75:             let validated_path = validation::validate_path(dir, ALLOWED_DIRS)?;\n76:             cmd.current_dir(validated_path);\n77:         }\n78: \n79:         let output = cmd.output().map_err(|e| format!(\"Failed: {}\", e))?;\n80:         let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);\n81:         let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);\n82: \n83:         Ok(format!(\"Git diff:\\n{}\\n{}\", stdout, stderr))\n84:     }\n85: \n86:     fn git_log(&self, path: Option<&str>) -> Result<String, String> {\n87:         let mut cmd = Command::new(\"git\");\n88:         cmd.arg(\"log\").arg(\"--oneline\").arg(\"-20\");\n89: \n90: String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);\n98: \n99:         Ok(format!(\"Git log:\\n{}\\n{}\", stdout, stderr))\n100:     }\n101: }\n102: \n103: #[async_trait]\n104: impl AgentTrait for CodeReviewerAgent {\n105:     fn agent_type(&self) -> &str {\n106:         \"code-reviewer\"\n107:     }\n108:     fn name(&self) -> &str {\n109:         \"Code Reviewer\"\n110:     }\n111:     fn description(&self) -> &str {\n112:         \"Code review and analysis\"\n113:     }\n114: \n115:     fn operations(&self) -> Vec<String> {\n116:         vec![\n117:             \"search\".to_string(),\n118:             \"count\".to_string(),\n119:             \"diff\".to_string(),\n120:             \"log\".to_string(),\n121:         ]\n122:     }\n123: \n124:     fn security_profile(&self) -> &SecurityProfile {\n125:         &self.profile\n126:     }\n127: \n128:     async fn execute(&self, task: AgentTask) -> Result<TaskResult, String> {\n129:         let result = match task.operation.as_str() {\n130:             \"search\" => self.search_code(task.path.as_deref(), task.args.as_deref()),\n131:             \"count\" => self.count_lines(task.path.as_deref()),\n132:             \"diff\" => self.git_diff(task.path.as_deref(), task.args.as_deref()),\n133:             \"log\" => self.git_log(task.path.as_deref()),\n134: allowed.join(\"log\");\n282:         std::fs::write(&inside, \"ok\").unwrap();\n283:         let outside = root.path().join(\"outside\");\n284:         std::fs::write(&outside, {\n45:             let path = entry?.path();\n46:             if path.extension().is_some_and(|e| e == \"md\") {\n47:                 std::fs::remove_file(&path)?;\n48:             }\n49:         }\n50:     }\n51:     std::fs::create_dir_all(&out_dir).with_context(|| format!(\"creating {}\", out_dir.display()))?;\n52: \n53:     for (agent_type, body) in &rendered {\n54:         let path = out_dir.join(format!(\"{agent_type}.md\"));\n55:         std::fs::write(&path, body).with_context(|| format!(\"writing {}\", path.display()))?;\n56:     }\n57: \n58:     println!(\"wrote {} agent definitions to {}\", rendered.len(), OUT_DIR);\n59:     Ok(())\n60: }\n61: \n62: fn check(out_dir: &Path, rendered: &BTreeMap<String, String>) -> Result<()> {\n63:     let mut stale = Vec::new();\n64: \n65:     for (agent_type, body) in rendered {\n66:         let path = out_dir.join(format!(\"{agent_type}.md\"));\n67:         match std::fs::read_to_string(&path) {\n68:             Ok(on_disk) if &on_disk == body => {}\n69:             Ok(_) => stale.push(format!(\"{agent_type}: out of date\")),\n70:             Err(_) => stale.push(format!(\"{agent_type}: missing\")),\n71:         }\n72:     }\n73: \n74:     // Definitions on disk with no backing agent in the catalog.\n75:     if out_dir.exists() {\n76:         for entry in std::fs::read_dir(out_dir)? {\n77:             let path = entry?.path();\n78:             if path.extension().is_some_and(|e| e == \"md\")\n79:                 && let Some(stem) = path.file_stem().and_then(|s| s.to_str())\n80:                 && !rendered.contains_key(stem)\n81:             {\n82:                 stale.push(format!(\"{stem}: orphaned (not in catalog)\"));\n83:             }\n84:         }\n85:     }\n86: \n87:     if stale.is_empty() {\n88:         println!(\"{} agent definitions up to date\", rendered.len());\n89:         return Ok(());\n90:     }\n91: \n92:     eprintln!(\"agent definitions are stale; run `cargo run -p op-agents --bin gen-agent-defs`:\");\n93:     for s in &stale {\n94:         eprintln!(\"  {s}\");\n95:     }\n96:     std::process::exit(1);\n97: }\n98: \n99: /// Render one descriptor as markdown in the layout `md_parser` expects:\n100: /// YAML frontmatter (name, description, model) then `## Purpose`,\n101: /// `## Capabilities`, `## Behavioral Traits`, `## Knowledge Base`.\n102: fn render(d: &AgentDescriptor) -> String {\n103:     let mut out = String::new();\n104: \n105:     out.push_str(\"---\\n\");\n106:     out.push_str(&format!(\"name: {}\\n\", yaml_scalar(&d.agent_type)));\n107:     out.push_str(&format!(\"description: {}\\n\", yaml_scalar(&d.description)));\n108:     out.push_str(&format!(\"model: {DEFAULT_MODEL}\\n\"));\n109:     out.push_str(&format!(\"category: {:?}\\n\", d.category));\n110:     out.push_str(\"generated: true\\n\");\n111:     out.push_str(\"---\\n\\n\");\n112: \n113:     out.push_str(&format!(\"# {}\\n\\n\", d.name));\n114:     out.push_str(\n115:         \"<!-- Generated from crates/op-agents/src/agent_catalog.rs. Do not edit by hand;\\n     \\\n116:          edit the agent's Rust implementation and re-run gen-agent-defs. -->\\n\\n\",\n117:     );\n118: \n119:     out.push_str(\"## Purpose\\n\\n\");\n120:     out.push_str(&format!(\"{}\\n\\n\", d.description));\n121: \n122:     out.push_str(\"## Capabilities\\n\\n\");\n123:     if d.operations.is_empty() {\n124:         out.push_str(\"_No operations declared._\\n\\n\");\n125:     } else {\n126:         for op in &d.operations {\n127:             match d.schema_for(op) {\n128:                 Some(schema) => match op_detail(&schema.description, &d.description, op) {\n129:                     Some(detail) => out.push_str(&format!(\"- {op} — {detail}\\n\")),\n130:                     None => out.push_str(&format!(\"- {op}\\n\")),\n131:                 },\n132:                 None => out.push_str(&format!(\"- {op}\\n\")),\n133:             }\n134:         }\n135:         out.push('\\n');\n136:     }\n137: \n138:     out.push_str(\"## Behavioral Traits\\n\\n\");\n139:     out.push_str(&format!(\"- Category: {:?}\\n\", d.category));\n140:     out.push_str(&format!(\"- Agent type: {}\\n\\n\", d.agent_type));\n141: \n142:     out.push_str(\"## Knowledge Base\\n\\n\");\n143:     for op in &d.operations {\n144:         if let Some(schema) = d.schema_for(op) {\n145:             out.push_str(&format!(\n146:                 \"- `{}` input: {}\\n\",\n147:                 op,\n148:                 compact_json(&schema.input_schema)\n149:             ));\n150:         }\n151:     }\n152:     if d.operations.is_empty() {\n153:         out.push_str(\"- _None._\\n\");\n154:     }\n155: \n156:     out\n157: }\n158: \n159: /// Per-operation schema descriptions are built as\n160: /// `\"<agent description> — <detail>\"`, so reprinting them whole repeats the\n161: /// agent blurb on every bullet. Keep only the part that distinguishes the\n162: /// operation, and drop it entirely when nothing is left.\n163: fn op_detail(schema_description: &str, agent_description: &str, op: &str) -> Option<String> {\n164:     let trim_noise = |s: &str| s.trim_start_matches([' ', '—', '-']).trim().to_string();\n165: \n166:     let mut detail = trim_noise(\n167:         schema_description\n168:             .strip_prefix(agent_description)\n169:             .unwrap_or(schema_description),\n170:     );\n171:     // The remainder usually restates the operation name; the bullet already has it.\n172:     detail = trim_noise(detail.strip_prefix(op).unwrap_or(&detail));\n173: \n174:     (!detail.is_empty() && detail != agent_description).then_some(detail)\n175: }\n176: \n177: /// Quote a YAML scalar when it contains anything that would change the parse.\n178: fn yaml_scalar(s: &str) -> String {\n179:     let needs_quoting = s.is_empty()\n180:         || s.contains([':', '#', '\\n', '\"', '\\'', '{', '}', '[', ']', ','])\n181:         || s.starts_with(' ')\n182:         || s.ends_with(' ');\n183: \n184:     if needs_quoting {\n185:         format!(\"\\\"{}\\\"\", s.replace('\\\\', \"\\\\\\\\\").replace('\"', \"\\\\\\\"\"))\n186:     } else {\n187:         s.to_string()\n188:     }\n189: }\n190: \n191: /// One-line JSON so a schema never breaks the markdown list item.\n192: /// `simd_json`'s owned `Value` renders as compact JSON via `Display`.\n193: fn compact_json(v: &simd_json::OwnedValue) -> String {\n194:     v.to_string()\n195: }\n196: \n197: /// Walk up from the manifest dir to the workspace root (the dir holding `.agents`).\n198: fn repo_root() -> Result<PathBuf> {\n199:     let mut dir = PathBuf::from(env!(\"CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR\"));\n200:     loop {\n201:         if dir.join(\".agents\").exists() {\n202:             return Ok(dir);\n203:         }\n204:         if !dir.pop() {\n205:             anyhow::bail!(\"could not locate workspace root containing .agents/\");\n206:         }\n207:     }\n208: }"
}

type to PascalCase for D-Bus naming\n574: /// e.g., \"python-pro\" -> \"PythonPro\"\n575: fn to_pascal_case(s: &str) -> String {\n576:     s.split('-')\n577:         .map(|part| {\n578:             let mut chars = part.chars();\n579:             match chars.next() {\n580:                 None => String::new(),\n581:                 Some(first) => first.to_uppercase().chain(chars).collect(),\n582:             }\n583:         })\n584:         .collect()\n585: }\n586: \n587: /// Generate a unique agent ID\n588: pub fn   "source": "1: //! Dynamically assembled agents.\n2: //!\n3: //! Owner direction (2026-09-19): static agents are recreated dynamically, and\n4: //! the pieces they were made of are shared across agents with similar\n5: //! functions. A *piece* is one reusable fragment — a behaviour, a knowledge\n6: //! block, an example, or an executor capability — tagged by domain. The\n7: //! [`PieceLibrary`] is the shared pool; [`assemble`] composes a persona for one\n8: //! task from the pieces that fit that task's context. Nothing here is a fixed\n9: //! persona: `rust expert` and `D-Bus expert` are different selections from the\n10: //! same pool, and a fact stored in memory becomes a piece every agent can use.\n11: //!\n12: //! Seeds for the pool:\n13: //! - the unified static catalog (`GLOBAL_REGISTRY`): each agent's behaviour,\n14: //!   knowledge sections, examples and capabilities become pieces;\n15: //! - markdown agent definitions read through [`crate::generator::md_parser`];\n16: //! - memory entries (namespace `agent_pieces`) merged at runtime by the bridge.\n17: //!\n18: //! The assembler is deterministic and offline: scoring is lexical over domain\n19: //! tags and titles so it works with no vector store. When the caller already\n20: //! has semantic context (RAG snippets, memory hits), it passes them in\n21: //! [`TaskContext::snippets`] and they are rendered into the persona prompt.\n22: \n23: pub mod assemble;\n24: pub mod pieces;\n25: \n26: pub use assemble::{AssembledPersona, TaskContext, assemble};\n27: pub use pieces::{AgentPiece, PieceKind, PieceLibrary, PieceSource};"
}

24] = [\n416:         \"the\", \"and\", \"for\", \"with\", \"that\", \"this\", \"from\", \"into\", \"are\", \"you\", \"your\", \"use\",\n417:         \"using\", \"how\", \"what\", \"why\", \"does\", \"not\", \"can\", \"all\", \"any\", \"when\", \"our\", \"its\",\n418:     ];\n419:     let mut out: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n420:     for raw in text.split(|c: char| !c.is_alphanumeric() && c != '_') {\n421:         let word = raw.trim_matches('_').to_ascii_lowercase();\n422:         if word.len() < 3 || STOP.contains(&word.as_str()) {\n423:             continue;\n424:         }\n425:         if !out.contains(&word) {\n426:             out.push(word);\n427:         }\n428:     }\n429:     out\n430: }\n431: \n432: pub(crate) fn slug(text: &str) -> String {\n433:     text.chars()\n434:         .map(|c| {\n435:             if c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() {\n436:                 c.to_ascii_lowercase()\n437:             } else {\n438:                 '_'\n439:             }\n440:         })\n441:         .collect::<String>()\n442:         .trim_matches('_')\n443:         .to_string()\n444: }"

  "source": "1: //! Unified Agent Architecture\n2: //!\n3: //! This module implements the recommended architecture that:\n4: //! 1. Merges markdown prompts INTO Rust agents (single source of truth)\n5: //! 2. Clearly separates EXECUTION agents from PERSONA agents\n6: //! 3. Uses consistent naming: {type}-executor vs {type}-expert\n7: //!\n8: //! ## Agent Categories\n9: //!\n10: //! - **Execution Agents**: Can run code/commands with sandboxing\n11: //! - **Persona Agents**: LLM-only, provide expertise without code execution\n12: //! - **Orchestration Agents**: Coordinate other agents for complex workflows\n13: \n14: pub mod adapter;\n15: pub mod agent_trait;\n16: pub mod execution;\n17: pub mod legacy_catalog;\n18: pub mod orchestration;\n19: pub mod persona;\n20: pub mod prompts;\n21: pub mod registry;\n22: \n23: pub use adapter::UnifiedAgentAdapter;\n24: pub use agent_trait::{AgentCapability, AgentCategory, UnifiedAgent};\n25: pub use execution::ExecutionAgent;\n26: pub use orchestration::OrchestrationAgent;\n27: pub use persona::PersonaAgent;\n28: pub use registry::{GLOBAL_REGISTRY, UnifiedAgentRegistry};"
}

RwLock<HashMap<String, Arc<dyn UnifiedAgent>>>,\n18:     /// Agent factories\n19:     factories: HashMap<&'static str, fn() -> Box<dyn UnifiedAgent>>,\n20: }\n21: \n22: impl UnifiedAgentRegistry {\n23:     /// Create a new registry with all default agents\n24:     pub fn new() -> Self {\n25:         let mut factories: HashMap<&'static str, fn() -> Box<dyn UnifiedAgent>> = HashMap::new();\n26: \n27:         // Register all execution agents\n28:         for (id, factory) in EXECUTION_AGENTS.iter() {\n29:             factories.insert(*id, *factory);\n30:         }\n31: \n32:         // agents\n38:         for (id, factory) in ORCHESTRATION_AGENTS.iter() {\n39:             factories.insert(*id, *factory);\n40:         }\n41: \n42:         // Migrated legacy catalog IDs (overrides only when key already present)\n43:         for (id, factory) in super::legacy_catalog::migrated_factories() {\n44:             factories.entry(id).or_insert(factory);\n45:         }\n46: \n47:         Self {\n48:             agents: RwLock::new(HashMap::new()),\n49:             factories,\n50:         }\n51:     }\n52: \n53:     /// Resolve an agent id, accepting underscore or hyphen forms.\n54:     pub fn get_normalized(&self, id: &str) -> Option<Arc<dyn UnifiedAgent>> {\n55:         self.get(id).or_else(|| {\n56:             let alt = if id.contains('_') {\n57:                 id.replace('_', \"-\")\n58:             } else {\n59:                 id.replace('-', \"_\")\n60:             };\n61:             self.get(&alt)\n62:         })\n63:     }\n64: \n65:     /// Get an agent by ID (lazy loading)\n66:     pub fn get(&self, id: &str) -> Option<Arc<dyn UnifiedAgent>> {\n67:         // Check if already loaded\n68:         {\n69:             let agents = self.agents.read();\n70:             if let Some(agent) = agents.get(id) {\n71:                 return Some(Arc::clone(agent));\n72:             }\n73:         }\n74: \n75:         // Try to load from factory\n76:         if let Some(factory) = self.factories.get(id) {\n77:             let agent: Arc<dyn UnifiedAgent> = Arc::from(factory());\n78:             let mut agents = self.agents.write();\n79:             agents.insert(id.to_string(), Arc::clone(&agent));\n80:             return Some(agent);\n81:         }\n82: \n83:         None\n84:     }\n85: \n86:     /// List all available agent IDs\n87:     pub fn list_ids(&self) -> Vec<&str> {\n88:         self.factories.keys().copied().collect()\n89:     }\n90: \n91:     /// List agents by category\n92:     pub fn list_by_category(&self, category:    .collect()\n109:     }\n110: \n111:     /// Register a custom agent\n112:     pub fn register(&mut self, id: &'static str, factory: fn() -> Box<dyn UnifiedAgent>) {\n113:         self.factories.insert(id, factory);\n114:     }\n115: \n116:     /// Get count of registered agents\n117:     pub fn count(&self) -> usize {\n118:         self.factories.len()\n119:     }\n120: \n121:        counts\n135:     }\n136: }\n137: \n138: impl Default for UnifiedAgentRegistry {\n139:     fn default() -> Self {\n140:         Self::new()\n141:     }\n142: }\n143: \n144: /// Global registry
